def storage:
  stor0 is uint256 at storage 0
  counter is mapping of uint256 at storage 1
  orderStatus is mapping of struct at storage 2
  contractOffererNonce is mapping of uint256 at storage 3

def getOrderStatus(bytes32 orderHash): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 32
  return bool(uint8(orderStatus[orderHash].field_0)), 
         bool(uint8(orderStatus[orderHash].field_8)),
         Mask(120, 0, orderStatus[orderHash].field_0),
         Mask(120, 0, orderStatus[orderHash].field_0) >> 136

def getContractOffererNonce(address contractOfferer): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 32
  require not -Mask(96, 160, contractOfferer)
  return contractOffererNonce[address(contractOfferer)]

def getCounter(address offerer): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 32
  require not -Mask(96, 160, offerer)
  return counter[address(offerer)]

def incrementCounter(): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 0
  if stor0 - 1:
      revert with NoReentrantCalls()
  counter[caller] += uint128(block.hash(block.number - 1)) >> 128
  log CounterIncremented(
        uint256 newCounter=((uint128(block.hash(block.number - 1)) >> 128) + counter[caller]),
        address offerer=caller)
  return ((uint128(block.hash(block.number - 1)) >> 128) + counter[caller])

def matchOrders(tuple[] orders, tuple[] fulfillments) payable: 
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 64
  require orders <= LOCK8605463013()
  require orders + 35 <′ calldata.size
  require orders.length <= LOCK8605463013()
  require (32 * orders.length) + orders + 36 <= calldata.size
  require fulfillments <= LOCK8605463013()
  require fulfillments + 35 <′ calldata.size
  require fulfillments.length <= LOCK8605463013()
  require (32 * fulfillments.length) + fulfillments + 36 <= calldata.size
  mem[64] = (32 * uint32(cd[(uint32(orders) + 4)])) + 128
  mem[96] = uint32(cd[(uint32(orders) + 4)])
  idx = 0
  while idx < 32 * uint32(cd[(uint32(orders) + 4)]):
      _14 = mem[64]
      mem[64] = mem[64] + 512
      mem[_14] = _14 + 160

def matchAdvancedOrders(tuple[] orders, tuple[] criteriaResolvers, tuple[] fulfillments, address recipient) payable: 
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 128

def fulfillOrder(tuple order, bytes32 fulfillerConduitKey) payable:
